      "content":"/* src/012-dispatch.h — task dispatch layer, public API.\n *\n * What it is, in a sentence: the C function the thread pool runs\n * per task; reads inputs from the slot store, invokes the box's\n * language spec, pushes outputs to downstream input slots, and\n * spawns consumer tasks on input readiness.\n *\n * Designed in issue 304. As of this iteration: real input\n * reading, real spec invocation via per-worker handles, real\n * output pushing with FIFO + 1-cell peek slots, real\n * spawn-on-input-ready chaining under a single-spawn-per-box\n * rule. Iterator multi-spawn, the routing kinds beyond plain,\n * and the large-value heap path are still ahead.\n *\n * The `dispatch_ctx_t` bundle is the durable runtime state for\n * the whole run: graph, slot store, spec registry, pool, plus\n * a per-box \"ever spawned\" flag array and (optionally) a\n * per-box output-capture table for testing. Tasks carry a\n * pointer to the context so the action has everything it needs\n * without globals.\n */\n\n#ifndef SORAMECH_DISPATCH_H\n#define SORAMECH_DISPATCH_H\n\n#include <stdatomic.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n\n#include \"010-graph-loader.h\"\n#include \"011-spec-registry.h\"\n#include \"009-slot-store.h\"\n#include \"014-event-queue.h\"\n#include \"pool.h\"\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* {{{ Types */\ntypedef struct dispatch_ctx {\n    const graph_t        *graph;\n    slot_store_t         *slots;\n    spec_registry_t      *specs;\n    pool_t               *pool;\n\n    /* Diagnostic: incremented each time dispatch_action runs. */\n    _Atomic int           tasks_dispatched;\n\n    /* Per-box runtime state (spawn guard + optional output capture)\n     * lives in chunked-append chunks (see ctx_box_chunk_t in\n     * dispatch.c). The old flat-arrays-with-headroom model imposed\n     * a 4096-box runtime cap that issue 319's create_box could\n     * exceed; chunks remove the cap by growing on demand.\n     *\n     * Access goes through the private ctx_box_slot helper in\n     * dispatch.c; callers don't touch these directly. */\n    void                 *box_chunks_opaque;   /* ctx_box_chunk_t**, hidden type */\n    _Atomic unsigned int  n_box_chunks;\n    pthread_mutex_t       box_chunks_mu;\n    int                   capture_outputs;\n\n    /* Optional default output buffer size for invoke calls. If 0,\n     * defaults to 4096. */\n    int                   default_out_capacity;\n\n    /* Optional run-log event queue (issue 311). When non-NULL, the\n     * dispatch action emits task_start + task_end events through\n     * it. NULL turns the per-task logging off; the runner emits\n     * run_start / run_end on its own. */\n    event_queue_t        *events;\n\n    /* Verbose-event gate (issue 311 SORAMECH_LOG_VALUES=1). When\n     * 1 and `events` is non-NULL, dispatch_action also emits\n     * task_input / task_output events with the byte payloads\n     * (truncated to 4 KB per the architecture doc). */\n    int                   log_values;\n\n    /* Slot-event gate (issue 311 SORAMECH_LOG_SLOTS=1). Gates two\n     * categories: (a) the per-slot enumeration at startup that the\n     * pool runner emits after graph_attach_runtime, and (b) the\n     * per-push events emitted from push_one_connection (every push\n     * attempt, including skip paths with their reason). Together\n     * the two paint the complete slot-level timeline. Slot\n     * allocations from runtime_create_box ALWAYS emit when an\n     * events queue is present — they're rare and load-bearing. */\n    int                   log_slots;\n\n    /* Monotonic task id counter for run-log correlation. */\n    _Atomic int           next_task_id;\n} dispatch_ctx_t;\n\n/* Task structs are allocated from the unified allocator's slab\n * pool so spawn/free pairs amortise across the run instead of\n * paying per-call malloc bookkeeping. The chunk back-reference\n * lets dispatch_action release the cell via ua_unref without\n * having to look it up by data pointer. */\nstruct ua_chunk;\n\ntypedef struct dispatch_task {\n    int                   box_id;\n    int                   task_id;   /* assigned at dispatch_spawn time */\n    const dispatch_ctx_t *ctx;\n\n    /* 304 placeholder — live count of outgoing wires that still\n     * carry an unconsumed value pushed by this task. The box-\n     * retirement consumer that reads this lands with the\n     * lifetime-tracking slice; for now the field is set to 0 on\n     * spawn and otherwise unread. Keeping it in the layout\n     * documents the intent and lets a future slice flip the\n     * read-it-and-act-on-it switch without changing the struct\n     * shape callers depend on. */\n    uint32_t              live_wire_count;\n\n    struct ua_chunk      *chunk;     /* set by dispatch_spawn; freed by dispatch_action */\n} dispatch_task_t;\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ Lifecycle */\n/* Initialize the per-box runtime state owned by the context. The\n * graph + slots + specs + pool fields are caller-owned and just\n * referenced. Call after `graph_attach_runtime` so the per-box\n * runtime data is in place.\n *\n * `enable_output_capture` allocates `last_outputs` and\n * `last_output_sizes` so tests can inspect what each box wrote\n * after `pool_wait_quiescent`. */\nint  dispatch_ctx_init   (dispatch_ctx_t *ctx,\n                          const graph_t *g, slot_store_t *s,\n                          spec_registry_t *r, pool_t *p,\n                          int enable_output_capture, char **err);\n\n/* Free per-box runtime arrays; does not touch the externally-owned\n * graph / slots / specs / pool fields. */\nvoid dispatch_ctx_destroy(dispatch_ctx_t *ctx);\n\n/* Read a previously-captured output (issue 319 follow-on: callers\n * no longer touch the per-box arrays directly because those moved\n * into private chunked storage). Defaults\n     * to 1 (native) for box kinds whose output is plain bytes\n     * (read / write / create_box / connect — they don't carry a\n     * language and don't JSON-serialize). do_call_box overwrites\n     * with the spec's per-call decision. */\n    int output_native = 1;\n    if (out_buf && b) {\n        switch (b->kind) {\n            case BOX_CALL:\n                rc = do_call_box(ctx, b, task_id, out_buf, out_capacity,\n                                 &out_size, &output_native);\n                break;\n            case BOX_WRITE:\n                rc = do_write_box(ctx, b, task_id,\n                                  out_buf, out_capacity, &out_size);\n                break;\n            case BOX_CREATE_BOX:\n                rc = do_create_box_box(ctx, b, task_id,\n                                       out_buf, out_capacity, &out_size);\n                break;\n            case BOX_CONNECT:\n                rc = do_connect_box(ctx, b, task_id,\n                                    out_buf, out_capacity, &out_size);\n                break;\n            case BOX_READ:\n                /* 244: read boxes are pull-on-demand value sources;\n                 * dispatch_spawn_if_ready filters them out before\n                 * they ever reach this point. Reaching here is a\n                 * bug — log loudly. */\n                fprintf(stderr, \"dispatch: BUG — read box '%s' \"\n                                \"reached dispatch_action\\n\", b->id);\n                rc = -1;\n                break;\n            case BOX_MAP:\n                /* 248: encapsulated sub-map. The graph loader splices\n                 * the sub-map's boxes into the parent at load time and\n                 * orphans this box (no incoming wires after inlining).\n                 * Reaching here means either inlining didn't run or a\n                 * runtime path-B sub-graph dispatch was added later\n                 * without updating this case. For now, no-op\n                 * successfully so a stray dispatch doesn't crash. */\n                fprintf(stderr, \"dispatch: BOX_MAP '%s' reached dispatch_action; \"\n                                \"input-side inlining should have removed it\\n\",\n                                b->id);\n                out_size = 0;\n                rc = 0;\n                break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rc == 0 && b) {\n        capture(ctx, t->box_id, out_buf, out_size);\n        /* Verbose: emit the task's output payload before push (so\n         * the log entries land in computation order). */\n        if (ctx->log_values && ctx->events) {\n            event_queue_task_output(ctx->events, now_secs(),\n                                    task_id, out_buf, out_size);\n        }\n        /* Every kind pushes — even write, which emits its \"true\"\n         * boolean for any downstream wires (unwired output is just\n         * discarded, per the 229 unwired-output rule). */\n        push_routed(ctx, b, out_buf, out_size, output_native);\n        /* Iterator-style re-spawn: if this is a multi-spawn box and\n         * any POP input still has queued values, schedule another\n         * task on the same box. This is how an iterator \"loops\"\n         * over its input queue without the upstream needing to\n         * push twice. */\n        if (b->multi_spawn && box_pop_ready(ctx, t->box_id)) {\n            dispatch_spawn(ctx, t->box_id, 0);\n        }\n    } else if (b) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"dispatch: box '%s' failed (rc=%d)\\n\", b->id, rc);\n    }\n\n    long end_us = mono_us();\n    if (ctx->events) {\n        event_queue_task_end(ctx->events, now_secs(), task_id, worker_idx,\n                             end_us - start_us, out_size);\n    }\n\n    if (out_chunk) ua_unref(heap, out_chunk);\n    /* Release the task cell back to the slab. The chunk pointer\n     * was stashed on the task by dispatch_spawn. */\n    if (t->chunk) ua_unref(heap, t->chunk);\n\n    /* Clear the runtime-builtins TLS so a later task on this worker",

Matches the\n59\t * choice the pool runner uses so all events share one clock. */\n60\tstatic double rt_now_secs(void)\n61\t{\n62\t    struct timespec ts;\n63\t    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n64\t    return (double)ts.tv_sec + (double)ts.tv_nsec / 1e9;\n65\t}\n66\t/* }}} */\n67\t\n68\t/* {{{ Small error-formatting helper */\n69\t__attribute__((format(printf, 2, 3)))\n70\tstatic int set_err(char **err_out, const char *fmt, ...)\n71\t{\n72\t    if (!err_out) return -1;\n73\t    char buf[1024];\n74\t    va_list ap;\n75\t    va_start(ap, fmt);\n76\t    int n = vsnprintf(buf, sizeof buf, fmt, ap);\n77\t    va_end(ap);\n78\t    if (n < 0) { *err_out = NULL; return -1; }\n79\t    size_t len = (size_t)n < sizeof buf ? (size_t)n : sizeof buf - 1;\n80\t    char *out = malloc(len + 1);\n81\t    if (out) { memcpy(out, buf, len); out[len] = '\\0'; }\n82\t    *err_out = out;\n83\t    return -1;\n84\t}\n85\t/* }}} */\n86\t\n87\t/* {{{ Resolve a language name to its index in the spec registry */\n88\tstatic int find_spec_idx(struct spec_registry *r, const char *lang)\n89\t{\n90\t    int n = spec_registry_size(r);\n91\t    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n92\t        const lang_spec_t *s = spec_registry_at(r, i);\n93\t        if (s && s->name && strcmp(s->name, lang) == 0) return i;\n94\t    }\n95\t    return -1;\n96\t}\n97\t/* }}} */\n98\t\n99\t/* {{{ Strdup-into-arena helper */\n100\tstatic const char *arena_strdup(json_arena_t *a, const char *s)\n101\t{\n102\t    /* The arena exposes parse/value helpers but not a raw strdup;\n103\t     * for runtime-created boxes we don't strictly need the arena\n104\t     * to own the string — graph_destroy doesn't free per-box id /\n105\t     * lang / etc. strings on the runtime path. Make a local copy\n205\t     * so we don't require the caller to null-terminate. */\n206\t    char *spec_dup = malloc((size_t)spec_len + 1);\n207\t    if (!spec_dup) { json_arena_destroy(arena); return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out of memory\"); }\n208\t    memcpy(spec_dup, spec_json, (size_t)spec_len);\n209\t    spec_dup[spec_len] = '\\0';\n210\t\n211\t    int json_err_off = 0;\n212\t    const char *json_err_msg = NULL;\n213\t    json_node_t *root = json_parse(arena, spec_dup, &json_err_off, &json_err_msg);\n214\t    free(spec_dup);\n215\t    if (!root || json_kind(root) != JSON_OBJECT) {\n216\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n217\t        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: spec is not a JSON object (%s)\",\n218\t                       json_err_msg ? json_err_msg : \"parse failed\");\n219\t    }\n220\t\n221\t    /* Required: kind. The \"already\n233\t     * initialised\" constraint surfaces as a runtime error when\n234\t     * the new box gets dispatched (worker handle NULL); the\n235\t     * compile pipeline's spec filter is what decides which langs\n236\t     * a worker init's, and that filter runs at graph load.\n237\t     * Workaround: include at least one static box of each lang\n238\t     * you intend to create_box at runtime. Lifting this is a\n239\t     * follow-on (on-demand spec init). */\n240\t    json_node_t *lang_n = json_object_get(root, \"lang\");\n241\t    if (!lang_n || json_kind(lang_n) != JSON_STRING) {\n242\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n243\t        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: 'lang' string required\");\n244\t    }\n245\t    const char *lang = json_string_value(lang_n);\n246\t    int spec_idx = find_spec_idx(specs, lang);\n247\t    if (spec_idx < 0) {\n248\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n249\t        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: lang='%s' not registered \"\n250\t                                \"in the spec registry\", lang);\n251\t    }\n252\t\n253\t    /* Required: ref + fn */\n254\t    json_node_t *ref_n = json_object_get(root, \"ref\");\n255\t    json_node_t *fn_n  = json_object_get(root, \"fn\");\n256\t    if (!ref_n || json_kind(ref_n) != JSON_STRING ||\n257\t        !fn_n  || json_kind(fn_n)  != JSON_STRING) {\n258\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n259\t        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: 'ref' and 'fn' strings required\");\n260\t    }\n261\t\n262\t    /* Optional id; auto-generate when absent. */\n263\t    char id_buf[BOX_ID_GEN_BUF_SIZE];\n264\t    const char *id_source;\n265\t    json_node_t *id_n = json_object_get(root, \"id\");\n266\t    if (id_n && json_kind(id_n) == JSON_STRING) {\n267\t        id_source = json_string_value(id_n);\n268\t    } else {\n269\t        if (box_id_generate(id_buf, sizeof id_buf) != 0) {\n270\t            json_arena_destroy(arena);\n271\t            return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: id generator failed\");\n272\t        }\n273\t        id_source = id_buf;\n274\t    }\n275\t\n276\t    /* Build inputs. */\n277\t    input_decl_t *inputs = NULL;\n278\t    int           n_inputs = 0;\n279\t    if (build_inputs(json_object_get(root, \"inputs\"),\n280\t                     &inputs, &n_inputs, err_out) != 0) {\n281\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n282\t        return -1;\n283\t    }\n284\t\n285\t    /* Allocate the box record. */\n286\t    box_t *b = calloc(1, sizeof *b);\n287\t    if (!b) {\n288\t        free(inputs);\n289\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n290\t        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out of memory (box)\");\n291\t    }\n292\t    b->id   = arena_strdup(NULL, id_source);\n293\t    b->kind = BOX_CALL;\n294\t    b->lang = arena_strdup(NULL, lang);\n295\t    b->ref  = arena_strdup(NULL, json_string_value(ref_n));\n296\t    b->fn   = arena_strdup(NULL, json_string_value(fn_n));\n297\t    b->returns = NULL;\n298\t    b->routing.kind      = ROUTING_PLAIN;\n299\t    b->routing.n_outputs = 1;\n300\t    b->output_capacity = 256;   /* slice-1 default; 0 (variable) not exposed yet */\n301\t    b->n_inputs  = n_inputs;\n302\t    b->inputs    = inputs;\n303\t    atomic_init(&b->n_connections, 0);\n304\t    atomic_init(&b->connections,   NULL);\n305\t    b->spec_idx  = spec_idx;\n306\t    b->counter_slot_id = -1;\n307\t    b->multi_spawn     = 0;\n308\t\n309\t    /* Allocate one input slot per input port. The slot store's\n310\t     * 319b growth machinery handles the runtime-add path. */\n311\t    if (n_inputs > 0) {\n312\t        b->input_slot_ids   = malloc((size_t)n_inputs * sizeof(int));\n313\t        b->input_slot_modes = malloc((size_t)n_inputs * sizeof(int));\n314\t        if (!b->input_slot_ids || !b->input_slot_modes) {\n315\t            free(b->input_slot_ids);\n316\t            free(b->input_slot_modes);\n317\t            free((void *)b->id);\n318\t            free((void *)b->lang);\n319\t            free((void *)b->ref);\n320\t            free((void *)b->fn);\n321\t            free(inputs);\n322\t            free(b);\n323\t            json_arena_destroy(arena);\n324\t            return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out of memory (slot arrays)\");\n325\t        }\n326\t        for (int i = 0; i < n_inputs; i++) {\n327\t            int sid = slot_alloc(slots, b->output_capacity, 1, 0);\n328\t            if (sid < 0) {\n329\t                /* On failure, free what we've allocated; the slot\n330\t                 * store's earlier-allocated slots stay around until\n331\t                 * graph teardown (no slot_free in this slice — 315\n332\t                 * territory). */\n333\t                free(b->input_slot_ids);\n334\t                free(b->input_slot_modes);\n335\t                free((void *)b->id);\n336\t                free((void *)b->lang);\n337\t                free((void *)b->ref);\n338\t                free((void *)b->fn);\n339\t                free(inputs);\n340\t                free(b);\n341\t                json_arena_destroy(arena);\n342\t                return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: slot_alloc failed for input %d\", i);\n343\t            }\n344\t            b->input_slot_ids[i]   = sid;\n345\t            b->input_slot_modes[i] = 0;  /* SLOT_MODE_PEEK */\n346\t            /* Issue 311 + 319: log each runtime slot allocation so\n347\t             * the JSONL transcript shows when the new box's input\n348\t             * slots came into existence. Opt-in via SORAMECH_LOG_SLOTS\n349\t             * (the queue silently ignores the call when no writer\n350\t             * was opened with that env var). */\n351\t            event_queue_slot_alloc(tls_events, rt_now_secs(), sid,\n352\t                                   b->output_capacity, 1,\n353\t                                   b->id, b->inputs[i].name);\n354\t        }\n355\t    }\n356\t\n357\t    /* Append the box to the graph. */\n358\t    int new_idx = graph_add_box(g, b);\n359\t    if (new_idx < 0) {\n360\t        /* Free the box record but not the slots (no slot_free in\n361\t         * this slice). */\n362\t        free(b->input_slot_ids);\n363\t        free(b->input_slot_modes);\n364\t        free((void *)b->id);\n365\t        free((void *)b->lang);\n366\t        free((void *)b->ref);\n367\t        free((void *)b->fn);\n368\t        free(inputs);\n369\t        free(b);\n370\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n371\t        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: graph_add_box failed\");\n372\t    }\n373\t\n374\t    /* Fill the caller's out_id buffer. */\n375\t    snprintf(out_id, out_id_size, \"%s\", b->id);\n376\t\n377\t    /* Issue 311 + 319: the JSONL transcript records every runtime\n378\t     * graph mutation so the trace from create_box → push fan-out is\n379\t     * inspectable after the fact. The event fires AFTER graph_add_box\n380\t     * so the publish is already visible to any downstream walker. */\n381\t    event_queue_box_create(tls_events, rt_now_secs(),\n382\t                           b->id, \"call\", b->lang, b->ref, b->fn);\n383\t\n384\t    json_arena_destroy(arena);\n385\t    return 0;\n386\t}\n387\t/* }}} */\n388\t\n389\t/* {{{ runtime_connect() */\n390\tint runtime_connect(const char *conn_json, int conn_len, char **err_out)\n391\t{\n392\t    if (err_out) *err_out = NULL;\n393\t\n394\t    struct graph *g = runtime_active_graph();\n395\t    if (!g) return set_err(err_out, \"connect: no active runtime context\");\n396\t    if (!conn_json || conn_len <= 0) {\n397\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: connection JSON is empty\");\n398\t    }\n399\t\n400\t    json_arena_t *arena = json_arena_create();\n401\t    if (!arena) return set_err(err_out, \"connect: out of memory (arena)\");\n402\t\n403\t    char *conn_dup = malloc((size_t)conn_len + 1);\n404\t    if (!conn_dup) { json_arena_destroy(arena); return set_err(err_out, \"connect: out of memory\"); }\n405\t    memcpy(conn_dup, conn_json, (size_t)conn_len);\n406\t    conn_dup[conn_len] = '\\0';\n407\t    int json_err_off = 0;\n408\t    const char *json_err_msg = NULL;\n409\t    json_node_t *root = json_parse(arena, conn_dup, &json_err_off, &json_err_msg);\n410\t    free(conn_dup);\n411\t    if (!root || json_kind(root) != JSON_OBJECT) {\n412\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n413\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: JSON is not an object (%s)\",\n414\t                       json_err_msg ? json_err_msg : \"parse failed\");\n415\t    }\n416\t\n417\t    json_node_t *from_n = json_object_get(root, \"from_box\");\n418\t    json_node_t *to_n   = json_object_get(root, \"to_box\");\n419\t    json_node_t *port_n = json_object_get(root, \"to_input\");\n420\t    json_node_t *branch_n = json_object_get(root, \"from_branch\");\n421\t    if (!from_n || json_kind(from_n) != JSON_STRING ||\n422\t        !to_n   || json_kind(to_n)   != JSON_STRING ||\n423\t        !port_n || json_kind(port_n) != JSON_STRING) {\n424\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n425\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: from_box / to_box / to_input \"\n426\t                                \"strings required\");\n427\t    }\n428\t\n429\t    const char *from_id = json_string_value(from_n);\n430\t    const char *to_id   = json_string_value(to_n);\n431\t    const char *port    = json_string_value(port_n);\n432\t\n433\t    int from_idx = graph_box_index(g, from_id);\n434\t    int to_idx   = graph_box_index(g, to_id);\n435\t    if (from_idx < 0) {\n436\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n437\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: no box named '%s'\", from_id);\n438\t    }\n439\t    if (to_idx < 0) {\n440\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n441\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: no box named '%s'\", to_id);\n442\t    }\n443\t\n444\t    const box_t *to_box_c = graph_box(g, to_idx);\n445\t    if (!to_box_c) {\n446\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n447\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: graph_box('%s') returned NULL\", to_id);\n448\t    }\n449\t\n450\t    /* Resolve the to_input name to an index in the destination's\n451\t     * inputs[] array. */\n452\t    int port_idx = -1;\n453\t    for (int i = 0; i < to_box_c->n_inputs; i++) {\n454\t        if (to_box_c->inputs[i].name &&\n455\t            strcmp(to_box_c->inputs[i].name, port) == 0) {\n456\t            port_idx = i;\n457\t            break;\n458\t        }\n459\t    }\n460\t    if (port_idx < 0) {\n461\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n462\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: box '%s' has no input port '%s'\",\n463\t                       to_id, port);\n464\t    }\n465\t\n466\t    /* Build the connection record. Strings are malloc'd so they\n467\t     * outlive this parse arena. */\n468\t    connection_t c = {0};\n469\t    /* The to_box / to_input strings are stored on the connection\n470\t     * for diagnostics; cast away the const since we own these\n471\t     * malloc'd copies. */\n472\t    char *to_box_copy   = malloc(strlen(to_id) + 1); strcpy(to_box_copy, to_id);\n473\t    char *to_input_copy = malloc(strlen(port)  + 1); strcpy(to_input_copy, port);\n474\t    c.to_box       = to_box_copy;\n475\t    c.to_input     = to_input_copy;\n476\t    c.from_branch  = NULL;\n477\t    if (branch_n && json_kind(branch_n) == JSON_STRING) {\n478\t        const char *br = json_string_value(branch_n);\n479\t        char *br_copy = malloc(strlen(br) + 1); strcpy(br_copy, br);\n480\t        c.from_branch = br_copy;\n481\t    }\n482\t    c.to_box_idx   = to_idx;\n483\t    c.to_input_idx = port_idx;\n484\t\n485\t    /* graph_box returns const; we need a non-const pointer for\n486\t     * box_add_connection. Re-fetch via the boxes array (we just\n487\t     * verified from_idx is valid). */\n488\t    box_t **boxes = (box_t **)((void *)graph_box(g, 0) ? NULL : NULL);\n489\t    /* The above trick is wrong — graph_box returns box_t* directly.\n490\t     * The const is in our function's signature, not in the storage.\n491\t     * Just cast. */\n492\t    box_t *from_box = (box_t *)graph_box(g, from_idx);\n493\t    if (!from_box) {\n494\t        free((void *)c.to_box);\n495\t        free((void *)c.to_input);\n496\t        free((void *)c.from_branch);\n497\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n498\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: graph_box('%s') returned NULL\", from_id);\n499\t    }\n500\t    (void)boxes;\n501\t\n502\t    int rc = box_add_connection(g, from_box, c);\n503\t    if (rc != 0) {\n504\t        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n505\t        free((void *)c.to_box);\n506\t        free((void *)c.to_input);\n507\t        free((void *)c.from_branch);\n508\t        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: box_add_connection failed\");\n509\t    }\n510\t    /* Issue 311 + 319: log the runtime wire so the JSONL transcript\n511\t     * records the connect → push order. Emitted AFTER the\n512\t     * connection is appended (and atomic-published) so any reader\n513\t     * acting on this event sees a consistent producer record. */\n514\t    event_queue_wire_add(tls_events, rt_now_secs(),\n515\t                         from_id,\n516\t                         c.from_branch,\n517\t                         to_id,\n518\t                         port);\n519\t    json_arena_destroy(arena);\n520\t    return 0;\n521\t}\n522\t/* }}} */\n523\t"

      "content":"/* src/018-runtime-builtins.c — runtime self-construction primitives.\n *\n * See 018-runtime-builtins.h for the contract. This file does the\n * actual parsing-and-instantiating; the heavy lifting is in the\n * existing graph_add_box / box_add_connection / slot_alloc /\n * spec_registry primitives. This module is the glue.\n *\n * Issue 319d slice 1 supports kind=call, lang=lua, routing.kind=plain\n * with simple { name, type } inputs. Other shapes return an error\n * naming what's not yet supported.\n */\n#include \"018-runtime-builtins.h\"\n#include \"010-graph-loader.h\"\n#include \"009-slot-store.h\"\n#include \"011-spec-registry.h\"\n#include \"013-jsonl-events.h\"\n#include \"014-event-queue.h\"\n#include \"017-box-id.h\"\n#include \"json.h\"\n#include \"lang-spec.h\"\n\n#include <stdarg.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n/* {{{ Thread-local active context */\nstatic __thread struct graph         *tls_graph;\nstatic __thread struct slot_store    *tls_slots;\nstatic __thread struct spec_registry *tls_specs;\nstatic __thread struct event_queue   *tls_events;\n\nvoid runtime_set_active_context(struct graph         *g,\n                                struct slot_store    *s,\n                                struct spec_registry *r,\n                                struct event_queue   *e)\n{\n    tls_graph  = g;\n    tls_slots  = s;\n    tls_specs  = r;\n    tls_events = e;\n}\n\nvoid runtime_clear_active_context(void)\n{\n    tls_graph  = NULL;\n    tls_slots  = NULL;\n    tls_specs  = NULL;\n    tls_events = NULL;\n}\n\nstruct graph         *runtime_active_graph(void)  { return tls_graph; }\nstruct slot_store    *runtime_active_slots(void)  { return tls_slots; }\nstruct spec_registry *runtime_active_specs(void)  { return tls_specs; }\nstruct event_queue   *runtime_active_events(void) { return tls_events; }\n\n/* now_secs: monotonic timestamp for event emission. Matches the\n * choice the pool runner uses so all events share one clock. */\nstatic double rt_now_secs(void)\n{\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    return (double)ts.tv_sec + (double)ts.tv_nsec / 1e9;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ Small error-formatting helper */\n__attribute__((format(printf, 2, 3)))\nstatic int set_err(char **err_out, const char *fmt, ...)\n{\n    if (!err_out) return -1;\n    char buf[1024];\n    va_list ap;\n    va_start(ap, fmt);\n    int n = vsnprintf(buf, sizeof buf, fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n    if (n < 0) { *err_out = NULL; return -1; }\n    size_t len = (size_t)n < sizeof buf ? (size_t)n : sizeof buf - 1;\n    char *out = malloc(len + 1);\n    if (out) { memcpy(out, buf, len); out[len] = '\\0'; }\n    *err_out = out;\n    return -1;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ Resolve a language name to its index in the spec registry */\nstatic int find_spec_idx(struct spec_registry *r, const char *lang)\n{\n    int n = spec_registry_size(r);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n        const lang_spec_t *s = spec_registry_at(r, i);\n        if (s && s->name && strcmp(s->name, lang) == 0) return i;\n    }\n    return -1;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ Strdup-into-arena helper */\nstatic const char *arena_strdup(json_arena_t *a, const char *s)\n{\n    /* The arena exposes parse/value helpers but not a raw strdup;\n     * for runtime-created boxes we don't strictly need the arena\n     * to own the string — graph_destroy doesn't free per-box id /\n     * lang / etc. strings on the runtime path. But to keep\n     * lifetimes uniform we copy via plain malloc and lean on\n     * graph_destroy to free runtime-created boxes' owned strings.\n     *\n     * Slice-1 simplification: we malloc each string individually\n     * and rely on the box's free at graph_destroy. Each string\n     * field that runtime-created boxes own is freed there. */\n    (void)a;\n    size_t len = strlen(s);\n    char  *p   = malloc(len + 1);\n    if (!p) return NULL;\n    memcpy(p, s, len + 1);\n    return p;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ Build input declarations from the JSON inputs array */\nstatic int build_inputs(const json_node_t *inputs_node,\n                        input_decl_t **out_inputs, int *out_n,\n                        char **err_out)\n{\n    if (!inputs_node) {\n        *out_inputs = NULL;\n        *out_n      = 0;\n        return 0;\n    }\n    if (json_kind((json_node_t *)inputs_node) != JSON_ARRAY) {\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: 'inputs' must be an array\");\n    }\n    int n = json_array_size(inputs_node);\n    if (n == 0) {\n        *out_inputs = NULL;\n        *out_n      = 0;\n        return 0;\n    }\n    input_decl_t *arr = calloc((size_t)n, sizeof *arr);\n    if (!arr) return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out of memory\");\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n        json_node_t *e = json_array_at(inputs_node, i);\n        if (!e || json_kind(e) != JSON_OBJECT) {\n            free(arr);\n            return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: inputs[%d] is not an object\", i);\n        }\n        json_node_t *name_n = json_object_get(e, \"name\");\n        json_node_t *type_n = json_object_get(e, \"type\");\n        if (!name_n || json_kind(name_n) != JSON_STRING) {\n            free(arr);\n            return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: inputs[%d].name missing\", i);\n        }\n        arr[i].name = arena_strdup(NULL, json_string_value(name_n));\n        arr[i].type = (type_n && json_kind(type_n) == JSON_STRING)\n                          ? arena_strdup(NULL, json_string_value(type_n))\n                          : NULL;\n        arr[i].literal = NULL;\n        arr[i].optional = 0;\n        /* Issue 246: per-port custom translation shim — optional. */\n        json_node_t *xlate_n = json_object_get(e, \"custom_translation\");\n        arr[i].custom_translation =\n            (xlate_n && json_kind(xlate_n) == JSON_STRING)\n                ? arena_strdup(NULL, json_string_value(xlate_n))\n                : NULL;\n    }\n    *out_inputs = arr;\n    *out_n      = n;\n    return 0;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ runtime_create_box() */\nint runtime_create_box(const char *spec_json, int spec_len,\n                       char *out_id, size_t out_id_size,\n                       char **err_out)\n{\n    if (err_out) *err_out = NULL;\n\n    struct graph         *g     = runtime_active_graph();\n    struct slot_store    *slots = runtime_active_slots();\n    struct spec_registry *specs = runtime_active_specs();\n    if (!g || !slots || !specs) {\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: no active runtime context \"\n                                \"(must be called from within a spec invoke)\");\n    }\n    if (!spec_json || spec_len <= 0) {\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: spec is empty\");\n    }\n    if (!out_id || out_id_size < BOX_ID_GEN_BUF_SIZE) {\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out_id buffer too small \"\n                                \"(need at least %u bytes)\",\n                       (unsigned)BOX_ID_GEN_BUF_SIZE);\n    }\n\n    /* Parse the JSON spec. We use a scratch arena that we destroy\n     * at the end of the function — the box record's string fields\n     * are deep-copied via malloc above, so the arena's lifetime\n     * doesn't need to outlive the parse. */\n    json_arena_t *arena = json_arena_create();\n    if (!arena) return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out of memory (arena)\");\n\n    /* json_parse expects null-terminated input. Make a local copy\n     * so we don't require the caller to null-terminate. */\n    char *spec_dup = malloc((size_t)spec_len + 1);\n    if (!spec_dup) { json_arena_destroy(arena); return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out of memory\"); }\n    memcpy(spec_dup, spec_json, (size_t)spec_len);\n    spec_dup[spec_len] = '\\0';\n\n    int json_err_off = 0;\n    const char *json_err_msg = NULL;\n    json_node_t *root = json_parse(arena, spec_dup, &json_err_off, &json_err_msg);\n    free(spec_dup);\n    if (!root || json_kind(root) != JSON_OBJECT) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: spec is not a JSON object (%s)\",\n                       json_err_msg ? json_err_msg : \"parse failed\");\n    }\n\n    /* Required: kind. Slice 1 — only \"call\" is supported. */\n    json_node_t *kind_n = json_object_get(root, \"kind\");\n    if (!kind_n || json_kind(kind_n) != JSON_STRING ||\n        strcmp(json_string_value(kind_n), \"call\") != 0) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: only kind='call' is supported \"\n                                \"in this slice (issue 319d)\");\n    }\n\n    /* Required: lang. Issue 319e extends supported langs to any\n     * spec that's already registered AND whose per-worker state\n     * has been initialised on the current worker. The \"already\n     * initialised\" constraint surfaces as a runtime error when\n     * the new box gets dispatched (worker handle NULL); the\n     * compile pipeline's spec filter is what decides which langs\n     * a worker init's, and that filter runs at graph load.\n     * Workaround: include at least one static box of each lang\n     * you intend to create_box at runtime. Lifting this is a\n     * follow-on (on-demand spec init). */\n    json_node_t *lang_n = json_object_get(root, \"lang\");\n    if (!lang_n || json_kind(lang_n) != JSON_STRING) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: 'lang' string required\");\n    }\n    const char *lang = json_string_value(lang_n);\n    int spec_idx = find_spec_idx(specs, lang);\n    if (spec_idx < 0) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: lang='%s' not registered \"\n                                \"in the spec registry\", lang);\n    }\n\n    /* Required: ref + fn */\n    json_node_t *ref_n = json_object_get(root, \"ref\");\n    json_node_t *fn_n  = json_object_get(root, \"fn\");\n    if (!ref_n || json_kind(ref_n) != JSON_STRING ||\n        !fn_n  || json_kind(fn_n)  != JSON_STRING) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: 'ref' and 'fn' strings required\");\n    }\n\n    /* Optional id; auto-generate when absent. */\n    char id_buf[BOX_ID_GEN_BUF_SIZE];\n    const char *id_source;\n    json_node_t *id_n = json_object_get(root, \"id\");\n    if (id_n && json_kind(id_n) == JSON_STRING) {\n        id_source = json_string_value(id_n);\n    } else {\n        if (box_id_generate(id_buf, sizeof id_buf) != 0) {\n            json_arena_destroy(arena);\n            return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: id generator failed\");\n        }\n        id_source = id_buf;\n    }\n\n    /* Build inputs. */\n    input_decl_t *inputs = NULL;\n    int           n_inputs = 0;\n    if (build_inputs(json_object_get(root, \"inputs\"),\n                     &inputs, &n_inputs, err_out) != 0) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Allocate the box record. */\n    box_t *b = calloc(1, sizeof *b);\n    if (!b) {\n        free(inputs);\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out of memory (box)\");\n    }\n    b->id   = arena_strdup(NULL, id_source);\n    b->kind = BOX_CALL;\n    b->lang = arena_strdup(NULL, lang);\n    b->ref  = arena_strdup(NULL, json_string_value(ref_n));\n    b->fn   = arena_strdup(NULL, json_string_value(fn_n));\n    b->returns = NULL;\n    b->routing.kind      = ROUTING_PLAIN;\n    b->routing.n_outputs = 1;\n    b->output_capacity = 256;   /* slice-1 default; 0 (variable) not exposed yet */\n    b->n_inputs  = n_inputs;\n    b->inputs    = inputs;\n    atomic_init(&b->n_connections, 0);\n    atomic_init(&b->connections,   NULL);\n    b->spec_idx  = spec_idx;\n    b->counter_slot_id = -1;\n    b->multi_spawn     = 0;\n\n    /* Allocate one input slot per input port. The slot store's\n     * 319b growth machinery handles the runtime-add path. */\n    if (n_inputs > 0) {\n        b->input_slot_ids   = malloc((size_t)n_inputs * sizeof(int));\n        b->input_slot_modes = malloc((size_t)n_inputs * sizeof(int));\n        if (!b->input_slot_ids || !b->input_slot_modes) {\n            free(b->input_slot_ids);\n            free(b->input_slot_modes);\n            free((void *)b->id);\n            free((void *)b->lang);\n            free((void *)b->ref);\n            free((void *)b->fn);\n            free(inputs);\n            free(b);\n            json_arena_destroy(arena);\n            return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: out of memory (slot arrays)\");\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < n_inputs; i++) {\n            int sid = slot_alloc(slots, b->output_capacity, 1, 0);\n            if (sid < 0) {\n                /* On failure, free what we've allocated; the slot\n                 * store's earlier-allocated slots stay around until\n                 * graph teardown (no slot_free in this slice — 315\n                 * territory). */\n                free(b->input_slot_ids);\n                free(b->input_slot_modes);\n                free((void *)b->id);\n                free((void *)b->lang);\n                free((void *)b->ref);\n                free((void *)b->fn);\n                free(inputs);\n                free(b);\n                json_arena_destroy(arena);\n                return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: slot_alloc failed for input %d\", i);\n            }\n            b->input_slot_ids[i]   = sid;\n            b->input_slot_modes[i] = 0;  /* SLOT_MODE_PEEK */\n            /* Issue 311 + 319: log each runtime slot allocation so\n             * the JSONL transcript shows when the new box's input\n             * slots came into existence. Opt-in via SORAMECH_LOG_SLOTS\n             * (the queue silently ignores the call when no writer\n             * was opened with that env var). */\n            event_queue_slot_alloc(tls_events, rt_now_secs(), sid,\n                                   b->output_capacity, 1,\n                                   b->id, b->inputs[i].name);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* Append the box to the graph. */\n    int new_idx = graph_add_box(g, b);\n    if (new_idx < 0) {\n        /* Free the box record but not the slots (no slot_free in\n         * this slice). */\n        free(b->input_slot_ids);\n        free(b->input_slot_modes);\n        free((void *)b->id);\n        free((void *)b->lang);\n        free((void *)b->ref);\n        free((void *)b->fn);\n        free(inputs);\n        free(b);\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"create_box: graph_add_box failed\");\n    }\n\n    /* Fill the caller's out_id buffer. */\n    snprintf(out_id, out_id_size, \"%s\", b->id);\n\n    /* Issue 311 + 319: the JSONL transcript records every runtime\n     * graph mutation so the trace from create_box → push fan-out is\n     * inspectable after the fact. The event fires AFTER graph_add_box\n     * so the publish is already visible to any downstream walker. */\n    event_queue_box_create(tls_events, rt_now_secs(),\n                           b->id, \"call\", b->lang, b->ref, b->fn);\n\n    json_arena_destroy(arena);\n    return 0;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ runtime_connect() */\nint runtime_connect(const char *conn_json, int conn_len, char **err_out)\n{\n    if (err_out) *err_out = NULL;\n\n    struct graph *g = runtime_active_graph();\n    if (!g) return set_err(err_out, \"connect: no active runtime context\");\n    if (!conn_json || conn_len <= 0) {\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: connection JSON is empty\");\n    }\n\n    json_arena_t *arena = json_arena_create();\n    if (!arena) return set_err(err_out, \"connect: out of memory (arena)\");\n\n    char *conn_dup = malloc((size_t)conn_len + 1);\n    if (!conn_dup) { json_arena_destroy(arena); return set_err(err_out, \"connect: out of memory\"); }\n    memcpy(conn_dup, conn_json, (size_t)conn_len);\n    conn_dup[conn_len] = '\\0';\n    int json_err_off = 0;\n    const char *json_err_msg = NULL;\n    json_node_t *root = json_parse(arena, conn_dup, &json_err_off, &json_err_msg);\n    free(conn_dup);\n    if (!root || json_kind(root) != JSON_OBJECT) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: JSON is not an object (%s)\",\n                       json_err_msg ? json_err_msg : \"parse failed\");\n    }\n\n    json_node_t *from_n = json_object_get(root, \"from_box\");\n    json_node_t *to_n   = json_object_get(root, \"to_box\");\n    json_node_t *port_n = json_object_get(root, \"to_input\");\n    json_node_t *branch_n = json_object_get(root, \"from_branch\");\n    if (!from_n || json_kind(from_n) != JSON_STRING ||\n        !to_n   || json_kind(to_n)   != JSON_STRING ||\n        !port_n || json_kind(port_n) != JSON_STRING) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: from_box / to_box / to_input \"\n                                \"strings required\");\n    }\n\n    const char *from_id = json_string_value(from_n);\n    const char *to_id   = json_string_value(to_n);\n    const char *port    = json_string_value(port_n);\n\n    int from_idx = graph_box_index(g, from_id);\n    int to_idx   = graph_box_index(g, to_id);\n    if (from_idx < 0) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: no box named '%s'\", from_id);\n    }\n    if (to_idx < 0) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: no box named '%s'\", to_id);\n    }\n\n    const box_t *to_box_c = graph_box(g, to_idx);\n    if (!to_box_c) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: graph_box('%s') returned NULL\", to_id);\n    }\n\n    /* Resolve the to_input name to an index in the destination's\n     * inputs[] array. */\n    int port_idx = -1;\n    for (int i = 0; i < to_box_c->n_inputs; i++) {\n        if (to_box_c->inputs[i].name &&\n            strcmp(to_box_c->inputs[i].name, port) == 0) {\n            port_idx = i;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    if (port_idx < 0) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: box '%s' has no input port '%s'\",\n                       to_id, port);\n    }\n\n    /* Build the connection record. Strings are malloc'd so they\n     * outlive this parse arena. */\n    connection_t c = {0};\n    /* The to_box / to_input strings are stored on the connection\n     * for diagnostics; cast away the const since we own these\n     * malloc'd copies. */\n    char *to_box_copy   = malloc(strlen(to_id) + 1); strcpy(to_box_copy, to_id);\n    char *to_input_copy = malloc(strlen(port)  + 1); strcpy(to_input_copy, port);\n    c.to_box       = to_box_copy;\n    c.to_input     = to_input_copy;\n    c.from_branch  = NULL;\n    if (branch_n && json_kind(branch_n) == JSON_STRING) {\n        const char *br = json_string_value(branch_n);\n        char *br_copy = malloc(strlen(br) + 1); strcpy(br_copy, br);\n        c.from_branch = br_copy;\n    }\n    c.to_box_idx   = to_idx;\n    c.to_input_idx = port_idx;\n\n    /* graph_box returns const; we need a non-const pointer for\n     * box_add_connection. Re-fetch via the boxes array (we just\n     * verified from_idx is valid). */\n    box_t **boxes = (box_t **)((void *)graph_box(g, 0) ? NULL : NULL);\n    /* The above trick is wrong — graph_box returns box_t* directly.\n     * The const is in our function's signature, not in the storage.\n     * Just cast. */\n    box_t *from_box = (box_t *)graph_box(g, from_idx);\n    if (!from_box) {\n        free((void *)c.to_box);\n        free((void *)c.to_input);\n        free((void *)c.from_branch);\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: graph_box('%s') returned NULL\", from_id);\n    }\n    (void)boxes;\n\n    int rc = box_add_connection(g, from_box, c);\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        json_arena_destroy(arena);\n        free((void *)c.to_box);\n        free((void *)c.to_input);\n        free((void *)c.from_branch);\n        return set_err(err_out, \"connect: box_add_connection failed\");\n    }\n    /* Issue 311 + 319: log the runtime wire so the JSONL transcript\n     * records the connect → push order. Emitted AFTER the\n     * connection is appended (and atomic-published) so any reader\n     * acting on this event sees a consistent producer record. */\n    event_queue_wire_add(tls_events, rt_now_secs(),\n                         from_id,\n                         c.from_branch,\n                         to_id,\n                         port);\n    json_arena_destroy(arena);\n    return 0;\n}\n/* }}} */\n",
      "numLines":523
    }
  },

The new box's count\n * is published last with release semantics so a reader seeing\n * count = N also sees box N-1 fully published into the index. */\nint graph_add_box(graph_t *g, box_t *box)\n{\n    if (!g || !box) return -1;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g->graph_mu);\n\n    uint32_t n   = atomic_load_explicit(&g->n_boxes,      memory_order_relaxed);\n    uint32_t cap = atomic_load_explicit(&g->box_capacity, memory_order_relaxed);\n\n    if (n + 1u > cap) {\n        uint32_t new_cap = cap == 0 ? 16u : cap * 2u;\n        box_t **new_boxes = calloc((size_t)new_cap, sizeof(box_t *));\n        if (!new_boxes) {\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g->graph_mu);\n            return -1;\n        }\n        box_t **old_boxes = atomic_load_explicit(&g->boxes, memory_order_relaxed);\n        if (old_boxes && n > 0) {\n            memcpy(new_boxes, old_boxes, (size_t)n * sizeof(box_t *));\n        }\n        struct stale_arr *st = malloc(sizeof *st);\n        if (!st) { free(new_boxes); pthread_mutex_unlock(&g->graph_mu); return -1; }\n        st->ptr  = old_boxes;\n        st->next = g->stale_box_arrs;\n        g->stale_box_arrs = st;\n        atomic_store_explicit(&g->boxes,        new_boxes, memory_order_release);\n        atomic_store_explicit(&g->box_capacity, new_cap,   memory_order_release);\n    }\n\n    box_t **boxes = atomic_load_explicit(&g->boxes, memory_order_relaxed);\n    boxes[n] = box;\n    atomic_store_explicit(&g->n_boxes, n + 1u, memory_order_release);\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g->graph_mu);\n    return (int)n;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ Runtime mutation — box_add_connection() (issue 319d)\n *\n * Appends one connection to box `b`'s connections array via the\n * same copy-and-publish discipline used for boxes: allocate a\n * new connections[] sized current+1, copy the old in, append the\n * new connection, atomic-store the new connections pointer, then\n * atomic-store the new count. The old connections array is parked\n * on stale_box_arrs (the list is generic, not box-specific) and\n * freed at graph_destroy. */\nint box_add_connection(graph_t *g, box_t *b, connection_t conn)\n{\n    if (!g || !b) return -1;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g->graph_mu);\n\n    int n_old = atomic_load_explicit(&b->n_connections, memory_order_relaxed);\n    connection_t *old = atomic_load_explicit(&b->connections, memory_order_relaxed);\n\n    connection_t *new_conns = malloc((size_t)(n_old + 1) * sizeof(connection_t));\n    if (!new_conns) { pthread_mutex_unlock(&g->graph_mu); return -1; }\n    if (old && n_old > 0) {\n        memcpy(new_conns, old, (size_t)n_old * sizeof(connection_t));\n    }\n    new_conns[n_old] = conn;\n\n    if (old) {\n        struct stale_arr *st = malloc(sizeof *st);\n        if (!st) { free(new_conns); pthread_mutex_unlock(&g->graph_mu); return -1; }\n        st->ptr  = old;\n        st->next = g->stale_box_arrs;\n        g->stale_box_arrs = st;\n    }\n\n    atomic_store_explicit(&b->connections,   new_conns, memory_order_release);\n    atomic_store_explicit(&b->n_connections, n_old + 1, memory_order_release);\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g->graph_mu);\n    return 0;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ Accessors */\nconst char *graph_name(const graph_t *g)         { return g ? g->name : NULL; }\nconst char *graph_description(const graph_t *g)  { return g ? g->description : NULL; }\nconst char *graph_entry_box_id(const graph_t *g) { return g ? g->entry_box_id : NULL; }\nconst char *graph_map_dir(const graph_t *g)      { return g ? g->map_dir : NULL; }\nint         graph_n_boxes(const graph_t *g)\n{\n    return g ? (int)atomic_load_explicit(&g->n_boxes, memory_order_acquire) : 0;\n}\n\nconst box_t *graph_box(const graph_t *g, int i)\n{\n    if (!g || i < 0) return NULL;\n    uint32_t n = atomic_load_explicit(&g->n_boxes, memory_order_acquire);\n    if ((uint32_t)i >= n) return NULL;\n    box_t **boxes = atomic_load_explicit(&g->boxes, memory_order_acquire);\n    return boxes[i];\n}\n\nconst box_t *graph_box_by_id(const graph_t *g, const char *id)\n{\n    if (!g || !id) return NULL;\n    for (int i = 0; i < g->n_boxes; i++) {\n        if (strcmp(g->boxes[i]->id, id) == 0) return g->boxes[i];\n    }\n    return NULL;\n}\n\nint graph_box_index(const graph_t *g, const char *id)\n{\n    if (!g || !id) return -1;\n    for (int i = 0; i < g->n_boxes; i++) {\n        if (strcmp(g->boxes[i]->id, id) == 0) return i;\n    }\n    return -1;\n}\n\nint graph_n_languages(const graph_t *g) { return g ? g->n_languages : 0; }\n\nconst char *graph_language(const graph_t *g, int i)\n{\n    if (!g || i < 0 || i >= g->n_languages) return NULL;\n    return g->languages[i];\n}\n\nint graph_n_entry_boxes(const graph_t *g) { return g ? g->n_entry_boxes : 0; }\n\nint graph_entry_box(const graph_t *g, int i)\n{\n    if (!g || i < 0 || i >= g->n_entry_boxes) return -1;\n    return g->entry_box_ids[i];\n}\n\nint graph_n_size_classes(const graph_t *g) { return g ? g->n_size_classes : 0; }\n\nint graph_size_class(const graph_t *g, int i)\n{\n    if (!g || i < 0 || i >= g->n_size_classes) return -1;\n    return g->size_classes[i];\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ resolve_spec_for_box() — find the spec index by box->lang */\n/* Returns the spec's index in the registry, or -1 if not found. */\nstatic int resolve_spec_for_box(const box_t *box, spec_registry_t *r)\n{",

Connections\n702\t * whose from_branch is NULL (plain) are skipped; connections whose\n703\t * from_branch equals `branch` fire. */\n704\tstatic int push_branch(dispatch_ctx_t *ctx, const box_t *b,\n705\t                       const char *branch,\n706\t                       const void *out_bytes, int out_size,\n707\t                       uint32_t tag, int output_native)\n708\t{\n709\t    /* The producer's connections array and its count are atomic\n710\t     * fields published by box_add_connection (issue 319d) with\n711\t     * memory_order_release. Reading them with matching acquire\n712\t     * semantics is the right paradigm — a concurrent worker\n713\t     * inside box_add_connection on this box could otherwise\n714\t     * split the snapshot (new count, stale array pointer) and\n715\t     * index past the end of an old array. */\n716\t    int n_c = atomic_load_explicit(&b->n_connections, memory_order_acquire);\n717\t    connection_t *cs = atomic_load_explicit(&b->connections, memory_order_acquire);\n718\t    int fired = 0;\n719\t    for (int i = 0; i < n_c; i++) {\n720\t        const connection_t *c = &cs[i];\n721\t        if (!c->from_branch) continue;\n722\t        if (strcmp(c->from_branch, branch) != 0) continue;\n723\t        if (push_one_connection(ctx, b, c, out_bytes, out_size, tag,\n724\t                                output_native) != 0)\n725\t            return -1;\n726\t        fired++;\n727\t    }\n728\t    return fired;\n729\t}\n730\t/* }}} */\n731\t\n732\t/* {{{ push_to_downstream() — fan output to every outgoing connection */\n733\t/* The push triggers spawn_if_ready on the consumer. Tag = 0 for\n734\t * the plain fan-out case; iterator routing uses push_branch with a\n735\t * non-zero tag. `output_native` is the per-call format flag from\n736\t * the producer's invoke (issue 312); it threads through so\n737\t * push_one_connection can route to the correct dual-ring side. */\n738\tstatic int push_to_downstream(dispatch_ctx_t *ctx, const box_t *b,\n739\t                              const void *out_bytes, int out_size,\n740\t                              int output_native)\n741\t{\n742\t    /* Atomic-acquire pair with box_add_connection's release\n743\t     * stores.       "content":"/* {{{ push_one_connection() — push to a single connection's slot */\n/* The `tag` is the ordering hint for cell-tagged consumer slots\n * (issue 304's iterator-ordering rule). For pushes from\n * non-iterator producers it's 0; for iterator pushes the routing\n * code passes the counter value so parallel iterator tasks\n * preserve invocation order at the consumer.\n *\n * `conn_idx` is the producer-side connection index (which entry in\n * `b->connections` this is) — used to look up\n * `b->output_edge_native[conn_idx]` when the consumer's slot is\n * dual-ring (issue 312 slice 3). For producers that don't carry\n * per-edge classification (read / write / data boxes — no\n * `output_edge_native` array), the bit defaults to 0 (treated as\n * cross-language; the JSON ring receives the push). */\nstatic int push_one_connection(dispatch_ctx_t *ctx, const box_t *b,\n                               const connection_t *c,\n                               const void *out_bytes, int out_size,\n                               uint32_t tag,\n                               int output_native)\n{\n    /* Issue 311 + 319 diagnosis: every push_one_connection attempt\n     * gets logged when SORAMECH_LOG_SLOTS=1, with a short result\n     * string. The skip paths name *why* a push was skipped so a\n     * race like 319's \"input_slot_ids reads as NULL even after\n     * runtime_create_box populated it\" shows up in the JSONL as a\n     * \"input-slots-null\" push event instead of as a phantom\n     * missing event. */\n    int log_pushes = ctx->log_slots && ctx->events;\n    if (c->to_box_idx < 0) {\n        if (log_pushes) {\n            event_queue_push(ctx->events, now_secs(),\n                             b->id, c->to_box, c->to_input,\n                             -1, out_size, \"no-to-box-idx\");\n        }\n        return 0;\n    }\n    const box_t *dst = graph_box(ctx->graph, c->to_box_idx);\n    if (!dst) {\n        if (log_pushes) {\n            event_queue_push(ctx->events, now_secs(),\n                             b->id, c->to_box, c->to_input,\n                             -1, out_size, \"dst-null\");\n        }\n        return 0;\n    }\n    if (!dst->input_slot_ids) {\n        if (log_pushes) {\n            event_queue_push(ctx->events, now_secs(),\n                             b->id, dst->id, c->to_input,\n                             -1, out_size, \"input-slots-null\");\n        }\n        return 0;\n    }\n    if (c->to_input_idx < 0 || c->to_input_idx >= dst->n_inputs) {\n        if (log_pushes) {\n            event_queue_push(ctx->events, now_secs(),\n                             b->id, dst->id, c->to_input,\n                             -1, out_size, \"to-input-out-of-range\");\n        }\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    int     dst_slot = dst->input_slot_ids[c->to_input_idx];\n    int32_t dst_flags = slot_flags(ctx->slots, dst_slot);\n    int     rc;\n\n    /* Ring choice on dual-ring slots: when the producer's per-call\n     * output_native is 0 (because some sibling consumer of this\n     * producer is cross-language), the bytes in out_bytes are JSON\n     * regardless of THIS wire's per-edge classification. We must\n     * push to the JSON ring so the consumer's slot_pop_ordered\n     * returns the correct which_ring tag and the consumer's spec\n     * runs json_to_native rather than treating the JSON bytes as\n     * its language's native form. (Issue 318 follow-on: the\n     * per-edge bit alone lies about a per-call decision.)\n     *\n     * When output_native is 1 the producer wrote native bytes\n     * intended for same-language consumers; cross-language\n     * consumers on the same producer get the per-edge bit's\n     * direction (push to JSON ring with a wrapper or fall back —\n     * the wrapper path is captured elsewhere). */\n    if (dst_flags >= 0 && (dst_flags & SLOT_FLAG_DUAL_RING)) {\n        int native_edge = wire_is_native(b, c);\n        int push_native = output_native && native_edge;\n        if (push_native) {\n            rc = slot_push_native(ctx->slots, dst_slot,\n                                  out_bytes, out_size, tag);\n        } else {\n            rc = slot_push_json(ctx->slots, dst_slot,\n                                out_bytes, out_size, tag);\n        }\n    } else {\n        rc = slot_push(ctx->slots, dst_slot, out_bytes, out_size, tag);\n    }\n\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        if (log_pushes) {\n            event_queue_push(ctx->events, now_secs(),\n                             b->id, dst->id, c->to_input,\n                             dst_slot, out_size, \"push-failed\");\n        }\n        fprintf(stderr, \"dispatch: '%s' → '%s'.%s: push failed\\n\",\n                b->id, dst->id, c->to_input);\n        return -1;\n    }\n    if (log_pushes) {\n        event_queue_push(ctx->events, now_secs(),\n                         b->id, dst->id, c->to_input,\n                         dst_slot, out_size, \"ok\");\n    }\n    dispatch_spawn_if_ready(ctx, c->to_box_idx, 0);\n    return 0;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ push_branch() — push only to outgoing connections matching `branch` */\n/* `branch` is one of \"lt\" / \"eq\" / \"gt\" / \"out_<n>\". Connections\n * whose from_branch is NULL (plain) are skipped; connections whose\n * from_branch equals `branch` fire. */\nstatic int push_branch(dispatch_ctx_t *ctx, const box_t *b,\n                       const char *branch,\n                       const void *out_bytes, int out_size,\n                       uint32_t tag, int output_native)\n{\n    /* The producer's connections array and its count are atomic\n     * fields published by box_add_connection (issue 319d) with\n     * memory_order_release. Reading them with matching acquire\n     * semantics is the right paradigm — a concurrent worker\n     * inside box_add_connection on this box could otherwise\n     * split the snapshot (new count, stale array pointer) and\n     * index past the end of an old array. */\n    int n_c = atomic_load_explicit(&b->n_connections, memory_order_acquire);\n    connection_t *cs = atomic_load_explicit(&b->connections, memory_order_acquire);\n    int fired = 0;\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_c; i++) {\n        const connection_t *c = &cs[i];\n        if (!c->from_branch) continue;\n        if (strcmp(c->from_branch, branch) != 0) continue;\n        if (push_one_connection(ctx, b, c, out_bytes, out_size, tag,\n                                output_native) != 0)\n            return -1;\n        fired++;\n    }\n    return fired;\n}\n/* }}} */\n\n/* {{{ push_to_downstream() — fan output to every outgoing connection */\n/* The push triggers spawn_if_ready on the consumer. Tag = 0 for\n * the plain fan-out case; iterator routing uses push_branch with a\n * non-zero tag. `output_native` is the per-call format flag from\n * the producer's invoke (issue 312); it threads through so\n * push_one_connection can route to the correct dual-ring side. */\nstatic int push_to_downstream(dispatch_ctx_t *ctx, const box_t *b,\n                              const void *out_bytes, int out_size,\n                              int output_native)\n{\n    /* Atomic-acquire pair with box_add_connection's release\n     * stores. See push_branch above for the longer rationale. */\n    int n_c = atomic_load_explicit(&b->n_connections, memory_order_acquire);\n    connection_t *cs = atomic_load_explicit(&b->connections, memory_order_acquire);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_c; i++) {\n        if (push_one_connection(ctx, b, &cs[i],\n                                out_bytes, out_size, 0, output_native) != 0)\n            return -1;\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n/* }}} */\n",
