```json
          "content":"# 320 / 321 / 322 — dense reference\n\n## States added to box_t\n\n```\n+ _Atomic int gather_flag    // 0 free, 1 held\n+ _Atomic int refcount       // workers + mutator's +1 hold\n- _Atomic int n_connections  // → plain int\n- _Atomic(connection_t *) connections   // → plain pointer\n```\n\n## States added to graph_t\n\n```\n  boxes : box_t **                       (today)\n→ boxes : _Atomic(box_t *) *             (each cell atomic)\n```\n\n## Tags added to ordering ring\n\n```\nSLOT_RING_NATIVE      = 0    (existing)\nSLOT_RING_JSON        = 1    (existing)\nSLOT_RING_RECONFIGURE = 2    (NEW; payload = box_t *)\nSLOT_RING_DELETE      = 3    (NEW; payload ignored / NULL)\n```\nOpen: collapse RECONFIGURE+NULL into one tag, or keep two.\n\n## Box kinds\n\n```\n- BOX_CREATE_BOX\n- BOX_CONNECT\n+ BOX_UTILITY        // absorbs both, adds reconfigure + delete\n```\nOpen: rename — `utility` | `spec_applier` | `mutate` | `control`.\n\n## Atomic op table\n\n| field                  | op            | mem order              | actor          |\n|------------------------|---------------|------------------------|----------------|\n| graph.boxes[id]        | store         | release                | mutator        |\n| graph.boxes[id]        | load          | acquire                | any worker     |\n| box.gather_flag        | CAS 0→1       | acq on win / rlx fail  | arriving worker|\n| box.gather_flag        | store 0       | release                | exiting worker |\n| box.refcount           | fetch_add 1   | acquire                | worker + mutator|\n| box.refcount           | fetch_sub 1   | release                | worker + mutator|\n| slot ordering head     | spinlock R/W  | per-slot lock          | producer/consumer|\n| slot data cell         | spinlock R/W  | per-slot lock          | producer/consumer|\n| box.n_connections      | plain         | hb via slot release    | mutator only   |\n| box.connections        | plain         | hb via slot release    | mutator only   |\n\n## Lifecycle invariants\n\n```\nLIVE(B)        ≡ graph.boxes[B.id] == B\nSUPERSEDED(B)  ≡ graph.boxes[B.id] != B\nFREEABLE(B)    ≡ SUPERSEDED(B) ∧ refcount(B) == 0\n                  AND the dropping thread owns the free\n```\n\n## Worker arrival — pseudocode\n\n```\nB = ld_acq(boxes[X])\nif !B: drop / push-target-gone; exit\nif !cas(B.gather, 0→1): YARQ(X); exit\nfa(B.refcount, +1)\n\npeek = [head(ordering[B.slot[i]]) for i in ports]\n\nif any peek[i].tag in {RECONFIGURE, DELETE}:\n    NEW = pop(B.slot[i]).payload        // box_t* or NULL\n    map_slots(NEW, B)                   // by port name\n    st_rel(boxes[X], NEW)               // or NULL\n    st_rel(B.gather, 0)\n    emit(box_reconfigure | box_delete + discards)\n    if NEW: YARQ(X)\n    if fs_rel(B.refcount, 1) == 1: free(B)\n    exit\n\nif any peek[i] is empty:\n    st_rel(B.gather, 0)\n    if fs_rel(B.refcount, 1) == 1 && SUPERSEDED(B): free(B)\n    exit\n\nvals = [pop(B.slot[i]) for i in ports]\nst_rel(B.gather, 0)\ninvoke(B.spec, vals) → out\npush_routed(B, out)\nif fs_rel(B.refcount, 1) == 1 && SUPERSEDED(B): free(B)\n```\n\n## Mutator's pre-publish work (on NEW)\n\n```\nNEW = build_from_spec(spec_json)\nNEW.id = OLD.id                          // forced for reconfigure\nfor p in NEW.inputs:\n    if p.name in OLD.inputs:\n        NEW.slot_ids[p] = OLD.slot_ids[p]      // reuse, keep queue\n    else:\n        NEW.slot_ids[p] = slot_alloc(...)      // fresh\nabandoned = {p in OLD.inputs : p.name ∉ NEW.inputs}\nNEW.spec_idx, .multi_spawn resolved here\nn_connections, connections written here (no atomic — pre-publish)\n```\n\n## Slot mapping cases\n\n| port name in OLD | port name in NEW | result                        |\n|------------------|------------------|-------------------------------|\n| yes              | yes              | reuse slot id; keep queued    |\n| no               | yes              | slot_alloc                    |\n| yes              | no               | abandoned → discard event     |\n| rename via field | n/a              | TBD (future); not auto-detected |\n\n## Utility-box dispatch decision\n\n```\nspec = parse(input.spec)\nend  = wire_endpoint(self.out)\nif spec.action == \"delete\":\n    assert spec.id == end.id (if both present)\n    push RING_DELETE → end.slot[end.port]\nelif spec.id is set AND graph.box_by_id(spec.id) exists:\n    target = graph.box_by_id(spec.id)\n    assert target == end                  // wire endpoint sanity\n    NEW = build(spec)\n    push RING_RECONFIGURE(NEW) → target.slot[end.port]\nelse:\n    new_id = spec.id ?? auto_gen()\n    NEW = build(spec)\n    install at graph slot directly (create path, fold 319f)\n    emit_value(new_id) on self.out\n```\nOpen: spec-content-driven dispatch (above) vs wire-endpoint-driven.\n\n## Wire semantics by op\n\n| op           | self.out ring | payload             | downstream sees     |\n|--------------|---------------|---------------------|---------------------|\n| create       | NATIVE/JSON   | new id string       | normal value        |\n| reconfigure  | RECONFIGURE   | box_t *             | mutator branch      |\n| delete       | DELETE        | (ignored / NULL)    | mutator delete path |\n\nOpen: single-channel (above) vs split ports (`id_out` for create,\n`_control` on target for reconfigure/delete).\n\n## JSONL events\n\n```\n+ box_reconfigure   {box_id, post_spec, task_id, worker_idx, ts}\n+ reconfigure_discard {box_id, port, count, sample[≤256], task_id, worker_idx, ts}\n+ box_delete        {box_id, task_id, worker_idx, ts}\n+ push.result := \"target-gone\"           // when boxes[id] == NULL at push\n```\n\n## Strict-delete knob (322)\n\n```\nSORAMECH_STRICT_DELETE = 0   (default; silent drop + JSONL)\nSORAMECH_STRICT_DELETE = 1   (push to deleted box fails the producer)\n```\nOpen: default direction.\n\n## 321 — runtime_connect rewrite\n\n```\nbefore:\n  runtime_connect(json) {\n    parse → connection_t c\n    box_add_connection(from, c)         // copy + atomic publish\n  }\n\nafter:\n  runtime_connect(json) {\n    parse → from_id, conn\n    OLD = graph.box_by_id(from_id)\n    NEW = clone(OLD)\n    append conn to NEW.connections\n    push RING_RECONFIGURE(NEW) → from.slot[<dedicated port>]\n  }\n```\n\nBehavioural delta: connect is async (effect lands on the\nnext worker visit to `from`). Open: same-task fast-path\nwhen caller IS the producer's invoke?\n\n## 322 — sugar wrappers\n\n```\nruntime_destroy_box(id) := runtime_apply_box_spec(id, \"{\\\"action\\\":\\\"delete\\\"}\")\nruntime_apply_box_spec(id, json):\n    OLD = graph.box_by_id(id) ?? error\n    NEW = parse_into_box(json, base=OLD)  // delete → NULL\n    push RING_RECONFIGURE(NEW) | RING_DELETE → OLD.slot[<port>]\n```\n\nBridges: `soramech.destroy_box` (lua), `soramech_destroy_box`\n(c/bash). All thin wrappers; same dispatch path.\n\n## Dataflow diagram — reconfigure\n\n```\n[user_box] --(json spec)--> [utility-box] --(box_t *, RECONFIGURE)--> target.slot[p]\n                                                                            │\n                                            target's next worker:           │\n                                              cas gather, fa refcount       │\n                                              peek → RECONFIGURE  ←─────────┘\n                                              pop box_t *\n                                              swap graph.boxes[target.id]\n                                              release gather, YARQ\n                                              fs refcount → maybe free OLD\n```\n\n## Dataflow diagram — delete\n\n```\n[user_box] --(json {\"action\":\"delete\"})--> [utility-box]\n                            │\n                            └──(RING_DELETE)──> target.slot[p]\n                                                       │\n                            target worker: swap to NULL; emit box_delete;\n                            free OLD on refcount drop\n```\n\n## Dataflow diagram — push-to-deleted\n\n```\nproducer Y fires → push_one_connection → ld_acq(boxes[X]) == NULL\n   → emit push{result=target-gone}\n   → strict ? fail producer : continue\n```\n\n## Files touched\n\n```\nsrc/009-slot-store.{c,h}        + RECONFIGURE/DELETE tags + ord helpers\nsrc/010-graph-loader.{c,h}      box_t fields; per-cell atomic boxes[];\n                                drop atomic on connections; refcount free\nsrc/012-dispatch.{c,h}          gather CAS, refcount, mutator branch,\n                                BOX_UTILITY case, free hook\nsrc/013-jsonl-events.{c,h}      3 new events + target-gone result\nsrc/014-event-queue.{c,h}       producer entrypoints for above\nsrc/018-runtime-builtins.{c,h}  runtime_apply_box_spec, destroy_box,\n                                connect rewrite\nlangs/{lua,c,bash}/spec.c       destroy_box bridge\n```\n\n## Implementation order\n\n```\n1.  boxes[] cell → _Atomic(box_t *)\n2.  + gather_flag, refcount on box_t (zero-init both paths)\n3.  wrap dispatch_action: CAS gather + fa refcount + fs on exit\n4.  refcount-driven free hook\n5.  RECONFIGURE/DELETE tags in slot store\n6.  mutator branch in dispatch_action\n7.  BOX_UTILITY kind; folds in CREATE_BOX + CONNECT\n8.  runtime_connect → utility path (321; drop _Atomic on connections)\n9.  runtime_destroy_box + bridges (322)\n10. strict-delete knob\n11. fixtures: 320 (6) + 322 (2)\n12. doc datapaths refresh\n```\n\n## Open items (decision pending)\n\n```\nF1  single-channel wire vs split ports per op\nF2  RING_DELETE vs RING_RECONFIGURE(NULL)\nF3  dispatch by spec content (lean) vs wire endpoint\nF4  BOX_UTILITY name\nF5  strict-delete default direction\nF6  connect: async (lean) vs same-task fast-path\nF7  per-cell atomic via type vs builtins (lean type)\nF8  slot_free as prereq, or accept leak (lean accept)\n```\n\n## Invariant cheat-sheet\n\n```\ngather held by ≤1 worker per box at any moment\npeek and pop on a port serialised by the gather flag\ngraph.boxes[id] swap is the linearisation point of a mutation\nqueued values on shared-name ports survive reconfigure\nqueued values on abandoned ports → discard event, freed with OLD\nproducer never holds gather; push order is independent of mutation order\nrefcount == 0 ∧ superseded → freer; mutator's +1 prevents premature free\n```\n",
```

## [126]

```json
{
  "cwd":"/mnt/mtwo/programs/sora/soramech",
  "type":"user",
  "timestamp":"2026-05-25T15:59:44.151Z",
  "gitBranch":"master",
  "version":"2.1.121",
  "sessionId":"f841ede1-ab42-4757-bba0-385e56b84f84",
  "isSidechain":false,
  "message":{
    "role":"user",
    "content":[{
        "tool_use_id":"toolu_01Ssmw9scVMsS5xtKqxd3fyJ",
        "type":"tool_result",
        "content":"File created successfully at: /tmp/soramech-320-321-322-mid.md"
      }]
  },
  "toolUseResult":{
    "type":"create",
    "structuredPatch":[],
    "filePath":"/tmp/soramech-320-321-322-mid.md",
    "content":"# 320 / 321 / 322 — mid-detail plan\n\nOne feature, one mechanism. 320 builds the box-swap path\nthrough a unified utility-box kind. 321 routes the\nconnections-append through it. 322 covers the user-facing\nshape of delete.\n\nFor the long-form reasoning see `/tmp/soramech-320-321-322-plan.md`.\nFor the bare reference see `/tmp/soramech-320-321-322-dense.md`.\n\n---\n\n## The mechanism in one paragraph\n\nThe per-id graph slot becomes the only mutation point.\nMutators build a fully-typed replacement box off the hot\npath, push its pointer into one of the target's input\nslots tagged RING_RECONFIGURE, and the target's next\nworker swaps the graph slot under a per-box gather lock.\nCreate / reconfigure / delete and connections-append all\ncollapse onto that one path.\n\n---\n\n## State changes\n\n### box_t\n\n```\n+ _Atomic int gather_flag      // 0 free, 1 held\n+ _Atomic int refcount         // workers + mutator's +1 hold\n- _Atomic int n_connections    // becomes plain int\n- _Atomic(connection_t *) connections   // becomes plain pointer\n```\n\n`gather_flag` serialises the peek-then-pop window on the\nbox's input ports. `refcount` governs the OLD record's\nfree after a swap.\n\n### graph_t\n\n```\n  boxes : box_t **                       (today; cells non-atomic)\n→ boxes : _Atomic(box_t *) *             (per-cell atomic)\n```\n\nPer-id atomic store is the swap. Per-id atomic load is the\nworker's box lookup. Release / acquire pair carries the\nhappens-before from \"mutator finished building NEW\" to\n\"reader observed NEW.\"\n\n### slot_store ordering ring\n\n```\nSLOT_RING_NATIVE       = 0\nSLOT_RING_JSON         = 1\nSLOT_RING_RECONFIGURE  = 2   (NEW; payload = box_t *)\nSLOT_RING_DELETE       = 3   (NEW; payload ignored / NULL)\n```\n\nPer-slot spinlock unchanged; the new tags reuse it.\n\n### box kinds\n\n```\n- BOX_CREATE_BOX\n- BOX_CONNECT\n+ BOX_UTILITY        // absorbs both, adds reconfigure + delete\n```\n\n---\n\n## Atomic ops\n\n| field             | actor                  | op          | order      |\n|-------------------|------------------------|-------------|------------|\n| graph.boxes[id]   | mutator                | store       | release    |\n| graph.boxes[id]   | any worker             | load        | acquire    |\n| box.gather_flag   | arriving worker        | CAS 0→1     | acq / rlx  |\n| box.gather_flag   | exiting worker         | store 0     | release    |\n| box.refcount      | worker + mutator       | fetch_add 1 | acquire    |\n| box.refcount      | worker + mutator       | fetch_sub 1 | release    |\n| slot rings        | producer / consumer    | per-slot lock | unchanged|\n\nThree lock-free states (slot, gather, refcount) plus the\nexisting per-slot spinlocks. Nothing else needs new\nsynchronisation.\n\n---\n\n## Lifecycle\n\n```\nLIVE(B)       ≡  graph.boxes[B.id] == B\nSUPERSEDED(B) ≡  graph.boxes[B.id] != B          (incl. NULL)\nFREEABLE(B)   ≡  SUPERSEDED(B) ∧ refcount(B) == 0\n```\n\nThe thread that drops refcount from 1 to 0 AND finds\nSUPERSEDED owns the free. The mutator's +1 hold across\nthe swap is what prevents premature free: every worker\nthat touched OLD has either left already (refcount drop)\nor is still holding +1; the mutator's own drop is the\nlast one if everyone else is gone.\n\n---\n\n## Worker arrival flow\n\n```\nB = ld_acq(boxes[X])\nif !B:        emit push-target-gone; exit\nif !cas(B.gather, 0→1):  YARQ(X); exit\nfa(B.refcount, +1)\n\npeek heads of every B.input_slot[i]\n\ncase any head is RECONFIGURE | DELETE:\n    pop the tagged cell → NEW (box_t * or NULL)\n    map NEW.input_slot_ids by port-name against B\n    st_rel(boxes[X], NEW)\n    st_rel(B.gather, 0)\n    emit box_reconfigure | box_delete + discard events\n    if NEW: YARQ(X)\n    if fs_rel(B.refcount, 1) == 1: free(B)\n    exit\n\ncase any head is empty:\n    st_rel(B.gather, 0)\n    if last drop && SUPERSEDED: free(B)\n    exit\n\ncase all heads are values:\n    pop one cell per port\n    st_rel(B.gather, 0)\n    invoke(B.spec, gathered) → out\n    push_routed(B, out)\n    if last drop && SUPERSEDED: free(B)\n    exit\n```\n\nTwo details worth keeping in mind. Step 1 (lookup) runs\nonce per fire; we never re-resolve mid-task even if a\nswap lands. The mutator's \"release gather, then YARQ\"\norder matters — the YARQ'd task will look up the new\npointer, so the release must happen-before the YARQ.\n\n---\n\n## Mutator's pre-publish work\n\n```\nNEW = build_from_spec(spec_json)\nNEW.id = OLD.id                              (forced; reconfigure)\nfor p in NEW.inputs:\n    if p.name in OLD.inputs:\n        NEW.slot_ids[p] = OLD.slot_ids[p]    (reuse, keep queue)\n    else:\n        NEW.slot_ids[p] = slot_alloc(...)\nabandoned = { p in OLD.inputs : p.name ∉ NEW.inputs }\nNEW.spec_idx, .multi_spawn, .n_connections, .connections set here\n```\n\nAll of this happens while the mutator holds the gather\nflag, before the atomic-store at graph.boxes[id]. Because\nthe swap is release-ordered, NEW's fields are visible to\nany reader that loads the new pointer.\n\nSlot mapping is name-based. Renames look like delete +\ncreate from the diff's perspective (queued values on the\nold name discard, the new name starts empty).\n\n---\n\n## Wire semantics by op\n\n| op           | wire ring          | payload         | downstream behaviour       |\n|--------------|--------------------|-----------------|----------------------------|\n| create       | NATIVE / JSON      | new id string   | normal value consumer      |\n| reconfigure  | RECONFIGURE        | box_t *         | mutator branch fires       |\n| delete       | DELETE             | NULL            | mutator delete branch      |\n\nSingle-channel design: the utility-box's output wire\ncarries the tagged payload directly for reconfigure /\ndelete; for create it carries the new id as a normal\nvalue. The target distinguishes by tag.\n\n---\n\n## Utility-box dispatch decision (inside its fire)\n\n```\nspec = parse(self.input.spec)\n\nif spec.action == \"delete\":\n    push RING_DELETE → wire_endpoint.slot[port]\n\nelif spec.id is set AND graph.box_by_id(spec.id) exists:\n    target = graph.box_by_id(spec.id)\n    assert target.id == wire_endpoint.box.id    (safety)\n    NEW = build(spec)\n    push RING_RECONFIGURE(NEW) → wire_endpoint.slot[port]\n\nelse:\n    new_id = spec.id ?? auto_gen()\n    NEW = build(spec)\n    graph_add_box(NEW); store at graph.boxes[new_id]\n    emit_value(new_id) on self.out             (create path)\n```\n\nDispatch by spec content, with the wire endpoint as a\nsanity check. The create path doesn't push through the\nwire as a control message — it installs the box\nimperatively and emits the id as a normal value on the\nwire, matching today's BOX_CREATE_BOX shape.\n\n---\n\n## JSONL events\n\n```\n+ box_reconfigure       { box_id, post_spec, task_id, worker_idx, ts }\n+ reconfigure_discard   { box_id, port, count, sample[≤256], task_id, worker_idx, ts }\n+ box_delete            { box_id, task_id, worker_idx, ts }\n+ push.result += \"target-gone\"\n```\n\nAll three new events fire from inside the mutator branch\nwhile the gather flag is held — they're ordered with the\nswap in the transcript.\n\n---\n\n## Connections (321)\n\nBefore:\n\n```\nruntime_connect(json):\n    parse → from_id, conn\n    box_add_connection(from, conn)          // copy + atomic publish\n```\n\nAfter:\n\n```\nruntime_connect(json):\n    parse → from_id, conn\n    OLD = graph.box_by_id(from_id)\n    NEW = clone(OLD); append conn to NEW.connections\n    push RING_RECONFIGURE(NEW) → from.slot[<control port>]\n```\n\n`_Atomic` qualifiers come off `n_connections` and\n`connections`. Old append-only contract collapses; any\nfield can now be mutated through the same path.\n\nBehavioural delta: connect becomes async. The wire\nappears on the next worker visit to the producer.\nSynchronous semantics are recoverable via a same-task\nfast-path; see open item.\n\n---\n\n## Delete (322)\n\nUser entrypoints:\n\n```\nsoramech.destroy_box(\"X\")           lua\nsoramech_destroy_box(\"X\", ...)      c / bash\nruntime_destroy_box(id)             internal\n```\n\nAll wrap `runtime_apply_box_spec(id, {\"action\":\"delete\"})`.\nPush-to-deleted-target by default emits the JSONL event\nand continues (silent drop); a knob promotes to hard\nerror.\n\n```\nSORAMECH_STRICT_DELETE=0    (default; silent drop + push.target-gone)\nSORAMECH_STRICT_DELETE=1    (producer task fails on push to NULL slot)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Three dataflow snapshots\n\nReconfigure:\n\n```\n[upstream] --(json spec)--> [utility-box] --(box_t*, RECONFIGURE)--> X.slot[p]\n                                                                          │\n                                          X's next worker peeks p ────────┘\n                                          pops box_t*\n                                          swaps graph.boxes[X.id]\n                                          releases gather; YARQ\n                                          refcount drop → maybe free OLD\n```\n\nDelete:\n\n```\n[upstream] --(json {\"action\":\"delete\"})--> [utility-box]\n                                                │\n                                                └──(DELETE)──> X.slot[p]\n                                                                    │\n                                       X's next worker swaps to NULL\n                                       emit box_delete; free OLD on drop\n```\n\nPush to a deleted target:\n\n```\nY fires → push_one_connection → ld_acq(boxes[X]) == NULL\n                              → emit push{result=target-gone}\n                              → strict? fail Y : continue\n```\n\n---\n\n## Files touched\n\n```\nsrc/009-slot-store.{c,h}        new tags; new push/peek/pop helpers\nsrc/010-graph-loader.{c,h}      box fields; per-cell atomic boxes[];\n                                drop atomic on connections; free helper\nsrc/012-dispatch.{c,h}          gather CAS, refcount, mutator branch,\n                                BOX_UTILITY case\nsrc/013-jsonl-events.{c,h}      3 new events + target-gone result\nsrc/014-event-queue.{c,h}       producer entrypoints for above\nsrc/018-runtime-builtins.{c,h}  runtime_apply_box_spec, destroy_box,\n                                connect rewrite\nlangs/{lua,c,bash}/spec.c       destroy_box bridge\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation order\n\n```\n1.  boxes[] cells → _Atomic(box_t *)\n2.  + gather_flag, refcount on box_t\n3.  wrap dispatch_action: CAS gather + refcount bump + drop on exit\n4.  refcount-driven free hook\n5. BOX_UTILITY kind (subsumes CREATE_BOX, CONNECT)\n8.  runtime_connect → utility path; drop _Atomic on connections    (321)\n9.  runtime_destroy_box + bridges                                   (322)\n10. strict-delete knob\n11. fixtures: six for 320, two for 322\n12. doc datapath refresh\n```\n\n---\n\n## Invariants\n\n- gather flag held by at most one worker per box\n- peek and pop on a port serialised inside one worker's gather window\n- graph.boxes[id] swap is the linearisation point of a mutation\n- queued values on shared-name ports survive reconfigure\n- queued values on abandoned ports → discard event, freed with OLD\n- producer never holds gather; push order independent of mutation\n- refcount==0 ∧ SUPERSEDED → freer (mutator's +1 prevents premature)\n\n---\n\n## Open items\n\n```\nF1  utility-box's output: single-channel mixed semantics\n    vs split ports (id_out for create, _control on target).\n    Lean single-channel.\n\nF2  delete tag: RING_DELETE explicit vs RING_RECONFIGURE(NULL).\n    Lean explicit RING_DELETE.\n\nF3  dispatch trigger: spec content authoritative vs wire endpoint.\n    Lean spec content, wire endpoint as sanity check.\n\nF4  BOX_UTILITY name. Per-id atomic load is the\nworker's box lookup. Release / acquire pair carries the\nhappens-before from \"mutator finished building NEW\" to\n\"reader observed NEW.\"\n\n### slot_store ordering ring\n\n```\nSLOT_RING_NATIVE       = 0\nSLOT_RING_JSON         = 1\nSLOT_RING_RECONFIGURE  = 2   (NEW; payload = box_t *)\nSLOT_RING_DELETE       = 3   (NEW; payload ignored / NULL)\n```\n\nPer-slot spinlock unchanged; the new tags reuse it.\n\n### box kinds\n\n```\n- BOX_CREATE_BOX\n- BOX_CONNECT\n+ BOX_UTILITY        // absorbs both, adds reconfigure + delete\n```\n\n---\n\n## Atomic ops\n\n| field             | actor                  | op          | order      |\n|-------------------|------------------------|-------------|------------|\n| graph.boxes[id]   | mutator                | store       | release    |\n| graph.boxes[id]   | any worker             | load        | acquire    |\n| box.gather_flag   | arriving worker        | CAS 0→1     | acq / rlx  |\n| box.gather_flag   | exiting worker         | store 0     | release    |\n| box.refcount      | worker + mutator       | fetch_add 1 | acquire    |\n| box.refcount      | worker + mutator       | fetch_sub 1 | release    |\n| slot rings        | producer / consumer    | per-slot lock | unchanged|\n\nThree lock-free states (slot, gather, refcount) plus the\nexisting per-slot spinlocks.
```
